Function local_coordinate(h, deadline, max_iter):
- n, target = 0.5n; h=project_density(h,target)
- matrices init, F, Mmax = max(F), best_c=Mmax*2/n.
- best_h copy.
- set top_m = max(8, min(64, n//5))? For n=450, 60. For n=900, 80? Use active count adapt.
- For iteration until deadline:
  - refresh top maybe each iter: top_idx = np.argpartition(F, -top_m)[-top_m:] sorted? We need weights.
  - top_vals=F[top_idx]; min top? weight = exp((top_vals - max_val)/scale). scale = max(max_top - min_top, 1e-6, Mmax*1e-4). Or weight=exp((top_vals - max_top)/tau). Use tau maybe Mmax*0.05? Since top entries close. We want weight active lags with high F. Use tau = max(1e-6, (Mmax - min_top)*0.2, 1e-3). Could just top_m uniform; active max maybe multiple. For gradient direction, uniform top_m may be okay. But previous used softmax. Use weights = ones / m maybe faster. However if one lag dominates, uniform top_m still includes others. Could weight by exp.
  - r = C[top_idx].T @ w. Because C shape (n,n) rows positions, cols lags. C[top_idx].T @ w gives n.
  - mean_r = r.mean()
  - direction δ = mean_r - r (so reduce sum? Since r=∑w C, sum δ =0. Weighted first-order change = ∑ δ_p r_p = ∑(mean-r)r = mean∑r - ∑r^2 ≤0, good.)
  - We can try coordinate pair from top positive δ (increases) and top negative δ (decreases). But δ values may correspond to fractional small changes. Need pick actual i/j and d. We can represent direction by best decrease of weighted sum: i = argmin r among h<1, j=argmax r among h>0. Then cap. But for exact max, use line search.
  - To diversify, create pools: pos_indices = np.argsort(delta)[:pool] (most increase) but respect cap >eps; neg_indices = np.argsort(-delta)[:pool] (most decrease). pool maybe 8. For each pair compute candidate d via line search and exact max, pick best improvement. If no improvement, maybe try random pairs from gradient plus random.
  - After accepting, update matrices, best.
  - If no improvement for some iterations, use random restarts of direction: add random to h? Or perform batch gradient projection.

def step_search(F, v, cap, dl):
    # minimize phi(d)=max(F + d v + (d^2 if k=dl else 0))
    # Use ternary? convex. Let lo=0, hi=cap.
    # We can first evaluate if phi(0) <= phi(cap) etc. But we can just ternary 20 times.
    for _ in range(20):
       m1=lo+(hi-lo)/3; m2=hi-(hi-lo)/3
       val1 = max(np.max(F + m1*v), F[dl]+m1*v[dl]+m1*m1 if dl else -inf)
       val2 similarly
       if val1 < val2: hi=m2 else lo=m1
    d=0.5(lo+hi)

Pseudo:
def pair_line_search(F, C, i, j, cap, currentM):
    if cap <= 1e-12: return None, currentM, None
    v = C[i] - C[j]
    dl = abs(i-j)
    M = currentM
    # Determine safe limit using current max + small tol.
    tol = 1e-10
    limit = cap
    # For all k with v[k] > 1e-12:
    pos = v > 1e-12
    if np.any(pos):
        # line limit: (M + tol - F[pos]) / v[pos], require F< M+tol maybe; if any F>=M+tol and v>0 => 0
        cand = (M + 1e-9 - F[pos]) / v[pos]
        cand = np.where(cand > 0, cand, 0.0)
        # For shared quadratic if dl in pos:
        shared = (dl < len(F))
        # We can just take min over lines; quadratic limit smaller/equal? If v positive, quadratic root <= line? For F+vd+d^2≤M => d <= line? Since d^2 positive, yes line limit safe. For v negative, no positive limit. For shared with v negative, line no limit but quadratic eventually positive; we should include shared quadratic root for any v.
        if shared:
            A = 1.0; B = v[dl]; Cc = F[dl] - (M + 1e-9)
            # solve d^2 + B d + Cc ≤0. If Cc <=0, positive root = (-B + sqrt(B*B -4Cc))/2 (because Cc negative => sqrt > |B|)
            disc = B*B - 4.0*Cc
            if disc >=0:
                root = (-B + np.sqrt(disc)) / 2.0
                if root > 0:
                    limit = min(limit, root)
        limit = min(limit, float(np.min(cand)) if cand.size else limit)
    # If safe limit too small, still try cap fractions; maybe move with small increase in max but net? Since M is current max, any increase of current max lags cannot lower max. If unique max with v>0, safe=0. For multiple max, if one increases, max increases. So no improvement. But due inactive? We require not exceed M tolerance. If safe=0, skip.
    if limit <= 1e-10:
        # Maybe due but line search could still improve if max lags not unique and safe let some increase? No, max would increase. Skip unless evaluate cap and see? Maybe if safe 0 for an active with v>0 but that active not actually max due numerical? F near max. Safe conservative. Could try small cap anyway? But likely no.
        return None, M, None
    # Candidate d values: fractions of limit and cap
    dvals = set-ish list: limit, 0.5*limit, 0.25, 0.125, 0.75*limit, cap, min(limit,0.05), ...
    # But if limit>cap, limit=cap.
    best_d=0; best_val=M; best_v? no v needed outside.
    # To reduce allocations, for each d compute val via vector.
    # Maybe evaluate 6 values.
    for d in ...:
       if d <=0: continue
       tmp = F + d * v
       val = tmp.max()
       if dl>0:
           s = F[dl] + d*v[dl] + d*d
           if s > val: val=s
       if val < best_val - 1e-12:
           best_val=val; best_d=d
    return best_d, best_val, v

But after accepting, update F = F+d*v; F[dl]+=d*d. Use v from before; no need recompute max via np.max? best_val from eval. Set M=best_val.

Pool selection:

If v positive but q? q positive worsens. Need v<0). So choose i,j minimizing C diff for active k(s). For a single active lag k, v[k]=C_i,k - C_j,k +? no linear. To decrease, choose i with small C_i,k and j with large C_j,k. That is gradient for that lag. Coordinate can reduce unique active. Thus per iteration:
- Determine active weights. If unique max, r = C[:,kmax] maybe. If multiple, weighted.
- pos_pool = smallest r (increase) top P, neg_pool largest r (decrease) top P. But if only need reduce active, maybe i = argmin r, j=argmax r.
- Try pairs from pos_pool x neg_pool (P=6) and evaluate safe line search. Pick best val improvement.
- If none, try random pairs from larger pools (e.g., 20 random combos) to avoid.
- If still none for e.g. 50 iterations, run batch gradient step.

Batch step function:
- Compute r weighted top.
- δ = r.mean() - r.
- Project δ to box [-h, 1-h] with sum zero using binary search λ: d = clip(δ - λ, -h, 1-h), sum d=0. Since δ zero mean, projection may have nonzero sum due box.
- Scale α max 1. Try α fractions (0.9,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.01 etc) ensuring h+α d within bounds (up to α≤1). Evaluate exact objective via evaluate_erdos_c? Need F_recompute via np.correlate or compute c. Since batch not maintaining exact C. After accept, recompute F,C and best. Use too often expensive. Limit: if n<=800, evaluate. We can use direct FPoly? Use np.correlate(h_new,1-h_new,'full')*dx. For n=900, correlate O810k, 10 evals=8M, okay if every 100 iters.
- Accept if improve; recompute matrices.

Free local search loop:
for iter in range(max_iter):
  if deadline break
  # maybe recompute M=np.max(F) occasionally.
  # select active based on F
  top_m = adapt: if max-Fnext > tol maybe top_m small. tol = 1e-6 + 1e-5*M.
  # Get sorted top? For weights, use argpartition.
  idx = np.argpartition(F, -top_m)[-top_m:]
  vals = F[idx]
  span = vals.max() - vals.min()
  if span < 1e-9: weights = ones
  else weights = np.exp((vals - vals.max())/max(span*0.25,1e-6))
  normalize.
  r = C[idx].T @ weights  # if weights shape (m,), C[idx] shape(m,n), T @ weights -> n.
  # Pools:
  # To increase, we need r small and h<1; to decrease r large and h>0.
  pos = np.where(h < 1 - 1e-9)[0]; neg = np.where(h > 1e-9)[0]
  pos_r = np.argsort(r)[?]; but argsort full n O(n logn) 450 fine. We can do every iter maybe 100k*450log ~ maybe. Could use argpartition.
  pos_pool = np.argpartition(r, P)[:P] then filter cap? Simpler argsort.
  neg_pool = np.argsort(-r)[:P]
  # Actually want smallest r for pos, largest for neg.
  best_improvement = -inf
  for i in pos_pool:
    if h[i] >=1: continue
    for j in neg_pool:
       if h[j]<=0 or i==j: continue
       cap = min(1-h[i], h[j])
       if cap<1e-10: continue
       d,val,v = pair_line_search(...)
       if d and val < best_val: store
  if stored: apply.
  else:
     # Try reverse? If we choose pos small r, neg large r. Maybe also choose top r? No.
     # Random pairs: choose i randomly from lowest 50 r with h<1, j from highest 50 with h>0.
     if stall%? try 20 random.
  if no improve: stall++
  if stall > 20: batch_step; stall=0
  else stall--
  # Periodically recompute F,C every 500 iters or when drift.

Objective for Fourier eval:
def make_fourier_h(a, n): x=(arange(n)+0.5)*2/n; y=zeros(n); for m in 1..M: y += a[m-1]*cos(mπ x); h=0.5+y; h=np.clip(h,0,1); return project_density(h,0.5*n)

   iter++
   if iter % 200 ==0 or drift: recompute FC? Maybe after 1000 recompute to correct. Recompute cost small.

For a position p, coeffP over k (constraints) is not a simple vector? But to get v at position i minus j, we need for each lag k: C_i^P[k] - C_j^P[k]. We can construct vP directly using contributions from two positions:
For P:
- From increasing i:
   k =0..n-i-1: += 1 - h[i+k]
   k=0..i: -= h[i-k]
- From decreasing j (subtraction of C_j):
   k=0..n-j-1: -= 1 - h[j+k]
   k=0..j: += h[j-k]
So vP[k] additive over these intervals. We can create zeros n and add slices:
   vP[:n-i] += 1 - h[i:]
   if i>0: vP[:i+1] -= h[i::-1]
   vP[:n-j] -= 1 - h[j:]
   if j>0: vP[:j+1] += h[j::-1]
Similarly vN:
   From i:
      k=0..i: += 1 - h[i-k] -> vN[:i+1] += 1 - h[i::-1]
      k=0..n-i-1: -= h[i+k] -> vN[:n-i] -= h[i:]
   From j subtract:
      vN[:j+1] -= 1 - h[j::-1]
      vN[:n-j] += h[j:]
This is O(n) vectorized (multiple slices). For line search, v = concatenate(vP, vN) (length 2n). q indices dl, n+dl.
F = concatenate(P, N). We maintain P,N arrays. Update P,N after accept with v: P += d*vP; N += d*vN; if dl: P[dl]+=d*d; N[dl]+=d*d. No C update! Great. Periodically recompute P,N to correct. This reduces complexity significantly.

Line search safe limit vectorized:
def pair_search(F, h, i, j, M):
 n=len(h); cap=min(1-h[i], h[j]); if cap<=1e-12 return None
 # compute vP,vN zeros; add slices as above
 vP=np.zeros(n); vN=np.zeros(n)
 # For P:
 vP[:n-i] += 1.0 - h[i:]
 if i>0: vP[:i+1] -= h[i::-1]
 vP[:n-j] -= 1.0 - h[j:]
 if j>0: vP[:j+1] += h[j::-1]
 # N:
 if i>0: vN[:i+1] += 1.0 - h[i::-1]
 vN[:n-i] -= h[i:]
 if j>0: vN[:j+1] -= 1.0 - h[j::-1]
 vN[:n-j] += h[j:]
 v = np.concatenate((vP, vN))
 dl=abs(i-j)
 # safe limit:
 limit=cap
 pos = v > 1e-12
 if np.any(pos):
    lim = (M + 1e-9 - F[pos]) / v[pos]
    lim = np.where(lim > 0, lim, 0)
    limit = min(limit, float(np.min(lim)))
 if dl > 0:
    for qi in (dl, n+dl):
       B=v[qi]; Cc=F[qi] - (M + 1e-9)
       if Cc > 1e-8: # should not
          # if B>0 no positive; if B<0 maybe? solve
          disc = B*B - 4*Cc
          if disc >=0: root=(-B+np.sqrt(disc))/2; limit=min(limit,root if root>0 else 0)
       else:
          disc = B*B - 4*Cc  # Cc<=0 => > B^2
          root = (-B + np.sqrt(max(disc,0.0))) * 0.5
          if root > 0: limit = min(limit, root)
 if limit <= 1e-9: return None
 # D values: use [limit, .75, .5, .25, .1, .05] scaled, maybe also cap? limit<=cap.
 mults=(1.0,0.75,0.5,0.35,0.2,0.08)
 D=np.array([limit*m for m in mults if limit*m > 1e-12], dtype=float)
 # Optionally deduplicate if limit tiny
 arr = F + D[:,None] * v[None,:]
 vals = arr.max(axis=1)
 if dl>0:
    s1 = F[dl] + D * v[dl] + D * D
    s2 = F[n+dl] + D * v[n+dl] + D * D
    shared = np.maximum(s1, s2)
    vals = np.maximum(vals, shared)
 # Also maybe evaluate a point from ternary? We can find min index.
 best_idx = int(np.argmin(vals))
 best_val=float(vals[best_idx]); best_d=float(D[best_idx])
 if best_d <=1e-12 or not (best_val < M - 1e-10): return None
 return best_d,best_val,v,vP,vN

def _set_fourier_eval(n, modes): global _F_N, _F_X, _F_MODES; ...
def fourier_h_from_params(raw, n, modes): # top-level, no global? can pass
   a = raw / max(1.0, np.sum(np.abs(raw))) * 0.99
   y = np.zeros(n)
   for idx,m in enumerate(modes): y += a[idx]*np.cos(np.pi*m*x)
   h=0.5+y; h=np.clip(h,0,1); return project_density(h,0.5*n)
def fourier_objective(raw):
   n=_F_N; x=_F_X; modes=_F_MODES; h=fourier_h_from_params(raw,n,x,modes); F=compute_F_all(h); return float(np.max(F)*(2.0/n))

Coordinate local time: We need implement robust max_iter/time. Use deadline in run, allocate fractions.

Let's design `coordinate_local(h, deadline, max_batch=...)`:
Inputs h, deadline, name. It will maintain best internally and return h,c.
Pseudo:
def coordinate_local(h, deadline, max_iter=None, top_frac=0.05, pool=8):
 start=time.perf_counter()
 n=h.size; target=0.5*n; h=project_density(h,target)
 F=compute_F_all(h); M=float(np.max(F)); dx=2.0/n; best_c=M*dx; best_h=h.copy()
 top_m = max(8, min(60, int(n*top_frac)))
 pool = max(4, min(10, int(top_m//5)+3))
 lag_count=0; stall=0; pert_fail=0
 while time.perf_counter() < deadline:
   # if M*dx < best_c: update
   M = max(M, float(np.max(F))) # guard
   if M*dx < best_c - 1e-12: best_c=M*dx; best_h=h.copy()
   # active r
   idx=np.argpartition(F, -top_m)[-top_m:]
   vals=F[idx]; maxv=vals.max(); minv=vals.min(); span=maxv-minv
   if span < 1e-8: w=np.full(top_m,1.0/top_m)
   else: tau=max(span*0.3,1e-7); w=np.exp((vals-maxv)/tau); w/=w.sum()
   r=compute_active_r(h, idx, w)
   # Pool selection
   pos_idx=np.argpartition(r, pool)[:pool]
   neg_idx=np.argpartition(r, -pool)[-pool:]
   # sort pos ascending, neg descending
   pos_idx=pos_idx[np.argsort(r[pos_idx])]
   neg_idx=neg_idx[np.argsort(-r[neg_idx])]
   # filter feasible
   pos_pool=[i for i in pos_idx if h[i] < 1-1e-8]
   neg_pool=[j for j in neg_idx if h[j] > 1e-8]
   # ensure lengths; if empty, random.
   best_pair=None; best_val=M
   # nested over up to pool*pool
   for i in pos_pool:
      for j in neg_pool:
         if i==j: continue
         d,val = pair_line_search(F,h,i,j,M)
         if d is not None and val < best_val - 1e-11:
             best_val=val; best_pair=(i,j,d)
             if val < M - 1e-7: break? Could break to save, but maybe better pair. We can continue all for best. pool small.
   if best_pair is None:
      # try random 12 pairs from broader pools
      broad_pos = np.argpartition(r, 30)[:30]; broad_neg=np.argpartition(r,-30)[-30:]
      filter feasible; for 12 random combos.
   if best_pair:
      i,j,d=best_pair
      apply_pair(h,F,i,j,d)
      M=best_val
      lag_count=0
      if M*dx < best_c: update

compute_vP_N function:
def pair_v(h,i,j):
 n=len(h); vP=zeros(n); vN=zeros(n)
 # P contributions as above
 if n-i >0: vP[:n-i] += 1.0 - h[i:]
 if i >0: vP[:i+1] -= h[i::-1]
 if n-j >0: vP[:n-j] -= 1.0 - h[j:]
 if j >0: vP[:j+1] += h[j::-1]
 # N
 if i >0: vN[:i+1] += 1.0 - h[i::-1]
 if n-i >0: vN[:n-i] -= h[i:]
 if j >0: vN[:j+1] -= 1.0 - h[j::-1]
 if n-j >0: vN[:n-j] += h[j:]
 return vP,vN

compute_F_all direct loops:
def compute_F_all(h):
 n=h.size; P=np.empty(n); N=np.empty(n)
 P[0]=np.sum(h*(1-h))
 N[0]=P[0]
 one_minus_h=1-h
 for k in range(1,n):
    P[k]=np.dot(h[:n-k], one_minus_h[k:])
    N[k]=np.dot(h[k:], one_minus_h[:n-k])
 return np.concatenate((P,N))

def batch_gradient_step(h, r, n, rng):

 for alpha in alphas:

    if out of bounds due numeric, project_density?
    h2 = project_density(h2, 0.5*n) # ensure sum/range

       accept

project_delta uses h and delta. It returns d with sum zero and box. The projection binary search:
def project_delta(delta, h):
 n=len(h)
 lo=-h; hi=1-h
 # initial lam
 lam_lo = float(np.min(delta)) - 1.0 - float(np.max(hi-lo))
 lam_hi = float(np.max(delta)) + 1.0 + float(np.max(hi-lo))
 for _ in range(60):
    mid=0.5*(lam_lo+lam_hi)
    d=np.clip(delta-mid, lo, hi)
    s=d.sum()
    if s > 0: # need larger mid
       lam_lo=mid
    else:
       lam_hi=mid
 d=np.clip(delta-0.5*(lam_lo+lam_hi), lo, hi)
 # correct residual sum by adjusting free? Projection should zero but due numerical.
 res=-d.sum()
 free=np.where((d>0)&(d<hi?))? Could distribute to free variables. Simpler: if abs(res)>1e-10: d += res/n; d=np.clip(d,lo,hi); then fine.
 return d

Let's write coordinate_local code in detail. Need pass rng for perturb/random.

Pseudo full function:
```
def coordinate_local(h, deadline, rng=None, top_frac=0.05, pool=8):
    if rng is None: rng = np.random.default_rng(123)
    n = h.size
    target = 0.5*n
    h = project_density(h, target)
    F = compute_F_all(h)
    M = float(np.max(F))
    dx = 2.0/n
    best_c = M*dx
    best_h = h.copy()
    top_m = max(8, min(50, int(n*top_frac)))
    pool = max(4, min(10, int(top_m//4)+3))
    no_improve=0
    iters=0
    while time.perf_counter() + 2.0 < deadline:
        iters += 1
        M = max(M, float(np.max(F)))
        if M*dx < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = M*dx; best_h = h.copy()
        # active
        idx = np.argpartition(F, -top_m)[-top_m:]
        vals = F[idx]
        maxv = float(vals.max()); minv=float(vals.min())
        span=maxv-minv
        if span < 1e-8:
            w = np.full(top_m, 1.0/top_m)
        else:
            tau = max(span*0.35, 1e-7)
            w = np.exp((vals - maxv)/tau)
            w /= w.sum()
        r = compute_active_r(h, idx, w, n)
        # pools
        br = np.where((h < 1.0) & (h > 0.0))[0]
        if br.size ==0: break
        # pos smallest r
        if br.size <= pool:
           pos_pool=br.tolist()
        else:
           pos_part = np.argpartition(r[br], pool)[:pool]
           pos_pool = br[pos_part]
           pos_pool = pos_pool[np.argsort(r[pos_pool])]
        if br.size <= pool:
           neg_pool=br.tolist()
        else:
           neg_part = np.argpartition(r[br], -pool)[-pool:]
           neg_pool = br[neg_part]
           neg_pool = neg_pool[np.argsort(-r[neg_pool])]
        best_pair=None; best_val=M
        # nested
        for i in pos_pool:
           ii=int(i)
           if h[ii] >= 1-1e-10 or h[ii] <= -1e-10: continue
           for j in neg_pool:
              jj=int(j)
              if ii==jj: continue
              if h[jj] <=1e-10: continue
              d,val = pair_line_search(F,h,ii,jj,M)
              if d is not None and val < best_val - 1e-10:
                 best_val=val; best_pair=(ii,jj,d)
        if best_pair is None:
           # random from broad top 25
           if br.size > 50:
              bpos = np.argpartition(r[br], 25)[:25]
              bpos = br[bpos]
              bneg = np.argpartition(r[br], -25)[-25:]
              bneg = br[bneg]
           else: bpos=br; bneg=br
           for _ in range(12):
              if bpos.size==0 or bneg.size==0: break
              ii=int(bpos[int(rng.integers(bpos.size))]); jj=int(bneg[int(rng.integers(bneg.size))])
              if ii==jj or not (0< h[jj] and h[ii]<1): continue
              d,val = pair_line_search(F,h,ii,jj,M)
              if d is not None and val < best_val -1e-10:
                 best_val=val; best_pair=(ii,jj,d)
        if best_pair is not None:
           ii,jj,d=best_pair
           apply_pair(h,F,ii,jj,d)
           M = float(np.max(F))  # exact
           if M*dx < best_c - 1e-12:
              best_c=M*dx; best_h=h.copy()
           no_improve=0
           # drift/sum check
           if iters % 200 ==0:
              s = float(np.sum(h)) - target
              if abs(s) > 1e-8:
                 h = project_density(h, target)
              F = compute_F_all(h); M=float(np.max(F))
              if M*dx < best_c -1e-12: update
        else:
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve % 20 == 0:
              # batch
              hb, Fb, Mb = batch_step(h,F,r,M)
              if hb is not None:
                 h=hb; F=Fb; M=Mb
                 if M*dx < best_c -1e-12: update
                 no_improve=0
           if no_improve > 100:
              # perturb
              improved=False
              for sigma in (0.03,0.02,0.01,0.005):
                 hp = project_density(h + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n), target)
                 Fp = compute_F_all(hp); Mp=float(np.max(Fp))
                 if Mp < M - 1e-8:
                    h=hp; F=Fp; M=Mp
                    if M*dx < best_c -1e-12: update
                    no_improve=0; improved=True; break
              if not improved and no_improve > 500:
                 # break? or keep batch. Maybe break to save time.
                 # Since local stuck, stop.
                 break
    return best_h, best_c
```

In apply_pair, update F with P,N. F length 2n-1. vP/vN from `pair_v`. Then if dl>0 update q. But if dl=0 skip not pair.
```
def apply_pair(h,F,i,j,d):
 n=len(h)
 vP,vN=pair_v(h,i,j)
 F[:n] += d*vP
 if n>1: F[n:] += d*vN[1:]
 dl=abs(i-j)
 if dl>0:
   F[dl] += d*d
   idxN = n+dl-1
   if idxN < F.size: F[idxN] += d*d
 h[i] += d
 h[j] -= d
```
Need F length 2n-1; if n=1 no. n>=2.

```
v[:n]=vP
if n>1: v[n:]=vN[1:]
```

`pair_v` returns vP and vN. For line search, after vC, compute safe limit.
```
def pair_line_search(F,h,i,j,M):
 n=len(h); cap=min(1.0-h[i], h[j]); if cap<=1e-12: return None
 vP,vN=pair_v(h,i,j)
 L=2*n-1; v=np.empty(L); v[:n]=vP; v[n:]=vN[1:]
 dl=abs(i-j)
 # safe
 limit=cap
 tol=1e-10
 pos = v > 1e-12
 if pos.any():
    lim = (M + 1e-9 - F[pos]) / v[pos]
    lim = np.where(lim > 0, lim, 0.0)
    # if any pos F >= M+1e-9, lim inf? formula negative ->0. If all pos? good.
    limit = min(limit, float(np.min(lim)))
 if dl>0:
    idxs=(dl, n+dl-1)
    for qi in idxs:
       B=float(v[qi]); Cc=float(F[qi]) - (M + 1e-9)
       if Cc <= 0:
          disc=B*B -4*Cc
          if disc <0: disc=0
          root = (-B + np.sqrt(disc))*0.5
          if root > 0: limit=min(limit, root)
       else:
          # F[qi] already above M (should not). solve if possible
          disc=B*B -4*Cc
          if disc >=0:
             root=(-B+np.sqrt(disc))*0.5
             if root >0: limit=min(limit,root)
             else: limit=0
 if limit <=1e-10: return None
 mults=(1.0,0.75,0.5,0.35,0.2,0.1,0.04)
 D=np.array([limit*m for m in mults if limit*m>1e-12], dtype=float)
 if D.size==0: return None
 # optional if D.max() > cap? limit<=cap
 vals = np.max(F + D[:,None]*v[None,:], axis=1)
 if dl>0:
   s1=F[dl]+D*v[dl]+D*D
   s2=F[n+dl-1]+D*v[n+dl-1]+D*D
   vals=np.maximum(vals, np.maximum(s1,s2))
 best_idx=int(np.argmin(vals)); best_d=float(D[best_idx]); best_val=float(vals[best_idx])
 # Could if best_val at D=limit and limit small, maybe try finer around? no.
 if best_d <=1e-12 or best_val >= M - 1e-11: return None
 return best_d, best_val
```

```
def compute_active_r(h, idx, w, n):
 r=np.zeros(n)
 for t in range(idx.size):
   ii=int(idx[t]); wi=float(w[t]); if wi==0 continue
   if ii < n: k=ii
   else: k=ii - n + 1
   if k <0: k=0
   if k >= n: continue
   m=n-k
   if k == 0:
      r += wi*(1.0 - 2.0*h)
   else:
      if m > 0:
         r[:m] += wi
         r[:m] -= wi*h[k:k+m]
      if k > 0:
         r[k:] -= wi*h[:m]
      # N part
      # p >= k
      r[k:] += wi
      r[k:] -= wi*h[:m]
      # p < m
      r[:m] -= wi*h[k:k+m]
 return r
```

```
def batch_step(h, F, r, M):
 n=h.size
 delta = r - r.mean(); delta = -delta  # or r.mean()-r
 d=project_delta(-delta? Wait delta_above = r.mean()-r. Use move = r.mean() - r.
 move = r.mean() - r
 d=project_delta(move,h)
 maxabs = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
 if maxabs <1e-12: return None
 s=d/maxabs
 for alpha in (1.0,0.6,0.35,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02):
    h2 = h + alpha*s*maxabs
    # h2 within bounds because d projection and alpha<=1
    h2 = np.clip(h2,0,1)
    h2 = project_density(h2,0.5*n)
    F2=compute_F_all(h2); M2=float(np.max(F2))
    if M2 < M - 1e-9:
       return h2,F2,M2
 return None,None,None
```

```
def make_basis(n, kind):
 x = (np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
 z = x-1.0
 B=[]
 if kind==0:
  for m in range(1,7): B.append(np.cos(np.pi*m*x))
 elif kind==1:
  B.append(z); B.append(0.5*(3*z*z-1)); ... up to 6
 elif kind==2:
  for m in [1,3,5,7,9]: B.append(np.cos(np.pi*m*x))
 else:
  B.append(z*z - 1/3)
  B.append(z**4 - 2/5)
  B.append(z**6 - 2/7)
 return B
```

```
def make_h_basis(raw, B, norms, n):
 denom = float(np.sum(np.abs(raw)*norms))
 scale = 0.5 / max(denom, 1e-12)

 for j in range(len(B)):
   f += scale*raw[j]*B[j]
 h = 0.5 + f
 # due scale, should be in [0,1]; clip
```

Compute_F_all:
```
def compute_F_all(h):
 n=h.size
 total=float(np.sum(h))
 A=np.correlate(h,h, mode='full')[n-1:2*n-2]  # length n-1, may be empty if n=1
 pref=np.empty(n+1); pref[0]=0; pref[1:]=np.cumsum(h)
 P=np.empty(n); N=np.empty(n)
 P[0]=np.sum(h*(1-h))
 N[0]=P[0]
 if n>1:
    P[1:] = pref[n-1:0:-1] - A  # pref indices n-1 down to1 length n-1
    N[1:] = (total - pref[1:n]) - A  # pref[1]..pref[n-1]
 F=np.empty(2*n-1); F[:n]=P; F[n:]=N[1:]
 return F
```
Check length: pref[n-1:0:-1] gives indices n-1,...,1 length n-1. total - pref[1:n] length n-1. Good.
This uses np.correlate O(n^2) C, much faster. Great.

Let's derive coefficient for N coefficient C^N_p[k] for increasing p.
N_k = ∑_{t=0}^{n-1-k} h_{t+k}(1 - h_t).
If increase p:
- If p = t+k (first factor): t=p-k >=0. Term h_p(1-h_{p-k}); Δ = d (1 - h_{p-k}). Condition p>=k.
- If p = t (second factor): t=p < n-k. Term h_{p+k}(1-h_p); Δ = -d h_{p+k}. Condition p < n-k.

Detailed run:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start=time.perf_counter()
    budget=float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 1300.0
    hard_start = start
    deadline = start + max(30.0, budget - 30.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_h=None; best_c=inf; best_n=0
    # parent
    try:
       raw=np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
       if raw.ndim==1 and raw.size>=2:
          raw=np.nan_to_num(raw,...); raw=np.clip(raw,0,1)
          h=project_density(raw,0.5*raw.size)
          c=evaluate_c(h)
          best_h=h; best_c=c; best_n=h.size
    except Exception: pass
    if best_h is None:
       n=450; x=...; h=0.5+0.65*np.cos(np.pi*x); h=project_density...
       best_c=evaluate_c(h); best_n=n
    parent_h=best_h.copy(); parent_c=best_c; parent_n=best_n
    # If very low budget, maybe return parent.
    # local parent if time rem > 120
    if deadline - time.perf_counter() > 120:
       dl = time.perf_counter() + min(180.0, (deadline-time.perf_counter())*0.15)
       h1,c1=coordinate_local(parent_h, dl, rng)
       if c1 < best_c - 1e-12:
           best_h=h1; best_c=c1; best_n=best_h.size
    # Choose fine n
    if budget > 1000: n_fine=900
    elif budget > 500: n_fine=720
    else: n_fine=450
    # Ensure n_fine even? dx, target 0.5n maybe .5 if odd. Parent likely even. Choose even to avoid half sum? Even better for target integer. If n_fine not even, set +=1.
    if n_fine %2 !=0: n_fine +=1
    if n_fine < parent_n: n_fine = parent_n
    # cap n_fine <=1080 maybe
    n_fine = min(max(300, n_fine), 1080)
    cand_h=None
    # basis search if time
    rem = deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if rem > 250:
       n_coarse = min(parent_n if parent_n>=300 else 450, n_fine)
       if n_coarse %2: n_coarse+=1
       cand_deadline = time.perf_counter() + min(240.0, rem*0.3)
       cands = basis_random_candidates(rng, n_coarse, cand_deadline)
       # add block candidates maybe within remaining
       # For each cand, evaluate (already), update best if better; choose fine start if better than parent
       fine_start=None
       for c,hc in cands:
          if c < best_c - 1e-12:
             best_h=hc; best_c=c; best_n=hc.size
          if fine_start is None or c < fine_start[0]:
             fine_start=(c,hc)
       if fine_start is not None and fine_start[0] < best_c + 0.01:
          cand_h = upsample_h(fine_start[1], n_fine)
       # block candidates?
       rem2=deadline-time.perf_counter()
       if rem2 > 100:
          block_deadline = time.perf_counter() + min(80, rem2*0.2)
          block_cands = block_random_candidates(rng,n_coarse,block_deadline)
          for c,hb in block_cands:
             if c < best_c -1e-12: update
             if fine_start is None or c < fine_start[0]: fine_start=(c,hb)
          if fine_start and (cand_h is None or fine_start[0] <= candidate_c_best? maintain):
             # If block best better than fine_start candidate, set cand_h
             pass
       # If no better candidate, use upsample current best
       if cand_h is None:
           cand_h = upsample_h(best_h, n_fine)
    else:
       cand_h = upsample_h(best_h, n_fine)
    cand_h = project_density(cand_h, 0.5*n_fine)
    # Evaluate cand c
    cand_c = evaluate_c(cand_h)
    if cand_c < best_c -1e-12: update
    # local fine
    rem = deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if rem > 60:
       h2,c2 = coordinate_local(cand_h, deadline - 2.0, rng)
       if c2 < best_c -1e-12:
          best_h=h2; best_c=c2; best_n=best_h.size
    # If fine not improved but parent local maybe, perhaps local parent at higher? Already.
    # If there is time and best_n != n_fine, maybe try local at best_n with remaining? coordinate_local can run.
    rem = deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if rem > 60 and best_h.size != cand_h.size:
       h3,c3=coordinate_local(best_h, deadline-2.0, rng)
       if c3 < best_c -1e-12: update
    # final project and evaluate
    final_h = project_density(best_h, 0.5*best_h.size)
    final_c = evaluate_c(final_h)
    if final_c < best_c: final_c=final_c
    return final_h, float(final_c), int(final_h.size)
```

`basis_random_candidates(rng,n_eval,deadline)`:
```
bases=[]
for kind in range(4):
   B=make_basis
   norms=np.array([np.max(np.abs(b)) for b in B])
   bases.append((B,norms))
cands=[]
# structured
for B,norms in bases:
   for s in range(8):
      raw = ...
      h=make_h_basis; c=evaluate_c; add
# random
t0=time.perf_counter(); T=max(30, deadline-t0)
bi=0
while time.perf_counter() < deadline:
   B,norms=bases[bi%4]; bi+=1
   # random raw
   if bi % 7 ==0:
      raw=np.zeros(len(B)); raw[int(rng.integers(len(B)))] = float(rng.uniform(-1,1))
   else:

      if len(cands) < 6000: cands.append((c,h))
```

```
def block_random_candidates(rng,n_eval,deadline):
 cands=[]
 while time<deadline and len(cands)<500:
   m=int(rng.integers(3,9))
   edges=np.sort(rng.random(m-1)*2.0)
   edges=np.concatenate(([0.0], edges, [2.0]))
   x=(np.arange(n_eval)+0.5)*2.0/n_eval
   idx=np.searchsorted(edges, x, side='right') - 1
   vals=rng.random(m)
   h=vals[idx]
   # overlap with mirror to encourage symmetry
   h=0.5*(h + h[::-1])
   h=project_density(h,0.5*n_eval)
   c=evaluate_c(h); if c<0.45 add
 return sorted top
```

Let's code `project_density` with direct shift:
```
def project_density(x, target):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).copy()
    x=np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    x=np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
    n=x.size
    if n==0: return x
    target=float(target)
    s=float(np.sum(x))
    if abs(s-target)<1e-12: return x
    shift=(target-s)/n
    h=x+shift
    if h.min() >= 0.0 and h.max() <= 1.0:
        # adjust tiny resid
        res=target-float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(res)>1e-12:
            free=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
            if free.any(): h[free]+=res/float(free.sum()); h=np.clip(h,0,1)
        return h
    lo,hi=-1.0,1.0
    for _ in range(70):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=float(np.sum(np.clip(x+mid,0,1)))
       if s<target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    h=np.clip(x+0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    res=target-float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(res)>1e-10:
       free=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
       if free.size:
          h[free]+=res/free.size; h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
If direct shift not feasible due bounds, binary. Good.

`evaluate_c`:
```
def evaluate_c(h):
 h=np.asarray(h,float); n=h.size
 if n==0: return inf
 dx=2.0/float(n)
 corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h, mode='full')
 return float(np.max(corr)*dx)
```
Could use `np.max(corr)` if corr all inf? no.

`compute_F_all`:
Need ensure `np.correlate(h,h,'full')` center slice. For n=1, full length1; slice [0:1]? `n-1:2*n-2` =0:0 empty. We'll handle if n>1.
```
def compute_F_all(h):
 n=h.size
 total=float(np.sum(h))
 P=np.empty(n); N=np.empty(n)
 P[0]=float(np.sum(h*(1-h)))
 N[0]=P[0]
 F=None
 if n==1:
   return P
 pref=np.empty(n+1); pref[0]=0.0; pref[1:]=np.cumsum(h)
 A=np.correlate(h,h,mode='full')
 A=A[n-1:2*n-2]
 P[1:]=pref[n-1:0:-1] - A
 N[1:]=total - pref[1:n] - A
 F=np.empty(2*n-1); F[:n]=P; F[n:]=N[1:]
 return F
```
For n=2, A slice [1:2] length1. pref[n-1:0:-1]=pref[1:0:-1] = [pref[1]] (h0). N: total-pref[1:2] = total-h0 = h1. Good.

`pair_v` uses h. Let's code with slices carefully.
```
def pair_v(h,i,j):
 n=len(h)
 vP=np.zeros(n); vN=np.zeros(n)
 # P add i
 vP[:n-i] += 1.0 - h[i:]
 if i > 0:
    vP[:i+1] -= h[i::-1]
 # P sub j
 vP[:n-j] -= 1.0 - h[j:]
 if j > 0:
    vP[:j+1] += h[j::-1]
 # N add i
 if i > 0:
    vN[:i+1] += 1.0 - h[i::-1]
 vN[:n-i] -= h[i:]
 # N sub j
 if j > 0:
    vN[:j+1] -= 1.0 - h[j::-1]
 vN[:n-j] += h[j:]
 return vP,vN
```
Check if i or j near n: n-i could be 0? i<n always; n-i>=1. n-j>=1. If i=0, h[i::-1]=h[0], but if i>0 condition for subtract for P, add N disabled? For P, second term for i=0: k=0 only -h0. Our code `if i>0: vP[:i+1] -= h[i::-1]` misses k=0 for i=0? Let's check P C_i for i=0: first k=0..n-1: 1-h[k]; second k=0: -h[0]. The first vP[:n] +=1-h[0:] includes +1-h0 at k0. Need subtract h0 at k0. Our `if i>0` skips, so wrong for i=0. Similarly N add i: first for i=0 none, second k=0..n-1: -h[0:] includes -h0. Our code `vN[:n-i] -= h[i:]` does it, okay. For P need include k=0 always: `vP[:i+1] -= h[i::-1]` even if i=0 (i+1=1, h[0::-1]=[h0]). So remove `if i>0`? For i=0, `h[0::-1]` good. For j=0, P subtract: `vP[:j+1] += h[j::-1]` = +h0 at k0 (subtract C_j second -h0). For N subtract j: `if j>0` first; for j=0, first none, second `vN[:n] += h[0:]` subtract C_j second? C_j for j=0 second -h[0:], subtract => +h[0:]. So we need P updates always for second/first? Let's generalize:
P add i: always `vP[:i+1] -= h[i::-1]` (length i+1)
P sub j: always `vP[:j+1] += h[j::-1]`
N add i: first if i>=0 always? first for k=0..i: 1-h[p-k], for i=0 k=0: 1-h0. Our code `if i>0` misses k0. Need always `vN[:i+1] += 1.0 - h[i::-1]`. But also second term `vN[:n-i] -= h[i:]` includes k0 -h0. At k0 total (1-h0)-h0=1-2h0 good. So always.
N sub j: first always `vN[:j+1] -= 1.0 - h[j::-1]`; second always `vN[:n-j] += h[j:]`.
P first: `vP[:n-i] += 1-h[i:]`; second always `vP[:i+1] -= h[i::-1]`.
P sub first: `vP[:n-j] -=1-h[j:]`; second: `vP[:j+1] += h[j::-1]`.
Thus no if except slice lengths automatically. For i=0, h[0::-1] length1. Good.
Let's test i=0 P: vP[:n]+=1-h; vP[:1]-=h0. At k0:1-h0-h0=1-2h0. Good. N: vN[:1]+=1-h0; vN[:n]-=h; at k0:1-h0-h0. Good.
Update pair_v accordingly.

```
def pair_v(h,i,j):
 n=len(h); vP=np.zeros(n); vN=np.zeros(n)
 vP[:n-i] += 1.0 - h[i:]
 vP[:i+1] -= h[i::-1]
```

```
def project_delta(delta, h):
 n=len(h); lo=-h; hi=1-h
 width=float(np.max(hi-lo))
 lam_lo=float(np.min(delta)) - width - 1.0
 lam_hi=float(np.max(delta)) + width + 1.0
 for _ in range(70): ...
 d = np.clip(delta - 0.5*(lam_lo+lam_hi), lo, hi)
 res = float(np.sum(d))
 if abs(res) > 1e-10:
   free = (d > 1e-12) & (d < 1.0 - 1e-12)  # delta bounds not h? d max <=1, min >=-1; free relative to hi/lo arrays better: (d > lo+1e-12)&(d < hi-1e-12)
   if free.size: d[free] -= res/free.size; d=np.clip(d,lo,hi)
 return d
```

```python
import time
import numpy as np


def project_density(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).copy()
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return x
    target = float(target)
    s = float(np.sum(x))
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-13:
        return x

    shift = (target - s) / float(n)
    h = x + shift
    if h.min() >= 0.0 and h.max() <= 1.0:
        res = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(res) > 1e-12:
            free = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
            if free.size > 0:
                h[free] += res / float(free.size)
                h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        return h

    lo = -1.0
    hi = 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(x + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(x + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    res = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(res) > 1e-10:
        free = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if free.size > 0:
            h[free] += res / float(free.size)
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def evaluate_c(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    if corr.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(np.max(corr) * dx)


def compute_F_all(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(np.sum(h * (1.0 - h)))], dtype=float)

    total = float(np.sum(h))
    P = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    N = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    P[0] = float(np.sum(h * (1.0 - h)))
    N[0] = P[0]

    A = np.correlate(h, h, mode="full")
    A = A[n - 1:2 * n - 2]

    pref = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    pref[1:] = np.cumsum(h)

    P[1:] = pref[n - 1:0:-1] - A
    N[1:] = total - pref[1:n] - A

    F = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    F[:n] = P
    F[n:] = N[1:]
    return F


def upsample_h(parent, n):
    p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float)
    n0 = p.size
    n = int(n)
    if n0 == n:
        return project_density(p.copy(), 0.5 * n)
    if n0 <= 0:
        return project_density(np.full(max(1, n), 0.5, dtype=float), 0.5 * n)
    x0 = (np.arange(n0) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n0))
    x1 = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    h = np.interp(x1, x0, p, left=float(p[0]), right=float(p[-1]))
    return project_density(h, 0.5 * n)


def compute_active_r(h, idx, w, n):
    r = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for a in range(idx.size):
        ii = int(idx[a])
        wi = float(w[a])
        if wi == 0.0:
            continue
        if ii < n:
            k = ii
            is_p = True
        else:
            k = ii - n + 1
            is_p = False
        if k < 0:
            k = 0
        if k >= n:
            continue
        m = n - k
        if is_p:
            if k == 0:
                r += wi * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
            else:
                if m > 0:
                    r[:m] += wi
                    r[:m] -= wi * h[k:k + m]
                if k > 0:
                    r[k:] -= wi * h[:m]
        else:
            if k == 0:
                r += wi * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
            else:
                r[k:] += wi
                r[k:] -= wi * h[:m]
                if m > 0:
                    r[:m] -= wi * h[k:k + m]
    return r


def pair_v(h, i, j):
    n = h.size
    vP = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    vN = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    vP[:n - i] += 1.0 - h[i:]
    vP[:i + 1] -= h[i::-1]

    vP[:n - j] -= 1.0 - h[j:]
    vP[:j + 1] += h[j::-1]

    vN[:i + 1] += 1.0 - h[i::-1]
    vN[:n - i] -= h[i:]

    vN[:j + 1] -= 1.0 - h[j::-1]
    vN[:n - j] += h[j:]

    return vP, vN


def pair_line_search(F, h, i, j, M):
    n = h.size
    cap = min(1.0 - float(h[i]), float(h[j]))
    if cap <= 1e-13:
        return None

    vP, vN = pair_v(h, i, j)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    v = np.empty(L, dtype=float)
    v[:n] = vP
    v[n:] = vN[1:]

    dl = abs(i - j)
    limit = cap
    level = M + 1e-10

    pos = v > 1e-13
    if np.any(pos):
        lim = (level - F[pos]) / v[pos]
        lim = np.where(lim > 0.0, lim, 0.0)
        limit = min(limit, float(np.min(lim)))

    if dl > 0:
        for qi in (dl, n + dl - 1):
            B = float(v[qi])
            Cc = float(F[qi]) - level
            disc = B * B - 4.0 * Cc
            if disc < 0.0:
                disc = 0.0
            root = 0.5 * (-B + float(np.sqrt(disc)))
            if root > 0.0:
                limit = min(limit, root)

    if not np.isfinite(limit) or limit <= 1e-12:
        return None

    mults = (1.0, 0.75, 0.55, 0.4, 0.25, 0.12, 0.05)
    D_list = []
    for m in mults:
        d = limit * m
        if d > 1e-13:
            D_list.append(float(d))
    if not D_list:
        return None
    D = np.array(D_list, dtype=float)

    vals = np.max(F + D[:, None] * v[None, :], axis=1)
    if dl > 0:
        s1 = F[dl] + D * v[dl] + D * D
        s2 = F[n + dl - 1] + D * v[n + dl - 1] + D * D
        vals = np.maximum(vals, np.maximum(s1, s2))

    bi = int(np.argmin(vals))
    bd = float(D[bi])
    bv = float(vals[bi])
    if bd <= 1e-13 or bv >= M - 1e-11:
        return None
    return bd, bv


def apply_pair(h, F, i, j, d):
    n = h.size
    vP, vN = pair_v(h, i, j)

    F[:n] += d * vP
    if n > 1:
        F[n:] += d * vN[1:]

    dl = abs(i - j)
    if dl > 0:
        F[dl] += d * d
        idxN = n + dl - 1
        if idxN < F.size:
            F[idxN] += d * d

    h[i] += d
    h[j] -= d
    if h[i] > 1.0:
        h[i] = 1.0
    elif h[i] < 0.0:
        h[i] = 0.0
    if h[j] > 1.0:
        h[j] = 1.0
    elif h[j] < 0.0:
        h[j] = 0.0


def project_delta(delta, h):
    n = h.size
    lo = -h
    hi = 1.0 - h
    width = float(np.max(hi - lo))
    lam_lo = float(np.min(delta)) - width - 1.0
    lam_hi = float(np.max(delta)) + width + 1.0

    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lam_lo + lam_hi)
        d = np.clip(delta - mid, lo, hi)
        s = float(np.sum(d))
        if s > 0.0:
            lam_lo = mid
        else:
            lam_hi = mid

    d = np.clip(delta - 0.5 * (lam_lo + lam_hi), lo, hi)
    res = float(np.sum(d))
    if abs(res) > 1e-10:
        free = (d > lo + 1e-12) & (d < hi - 1e-12)
        if free.size > 0:
            d[free] -= res / float(free.size)
            d = np.clip(d, lo, hi)
    return d


def batch_step(h, F, r, M):
    n = h.size
    move = r.mean() - r
    d = project_delta(move, h)
    maxabs = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if maxabs <= 1e-13:
        return None, None, None
    s = d / maxabs
    alphas = (1.0, 0.65, 0.4, 0.25, 0.13, 0.06, 0.025)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for alpha in alphas:
        h2 = h + alpha * s * maxabs
        h2 = np.clip(h2, 0.0, 1.0)
        h2 = project_density(h2, target)
        F2 = compute_F_all(h2)
        M2 = float(np.max(F2))
        if M2 < M - 1e-10:
            return h2, F2, M2
    return None, None, None


def coordinate_local(h, deadline, rng):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = h.size
    if n < 2:
        return h, evaluate_c(h)

    target = 0.5 * n
    h = project_density(h, target)
    F = compute_F_all(h)
    M = float(np.max(F))
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    best_c = M * dx
    best_h = h.copy()

    top_m = max(8, min(45, int(n * 0.045)))
    top_m = min(top_m, F.size)
    pool = max(3, min(5, int(top_m // 10) + 2))

    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    t_last_recalc = time.perf_counter()

    while time.perf_counter() + 1.5 < deadline:
        it += 1
        M = max(M, float(np.max(F)))
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(F)):
            F = compute_F_all(h)
            M = float(np.max(F))
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(F)):
                break

        if M * dx < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = M * dx
            best_h = h.copy()

        if top_m >= F.size:
            idx = np.arange(F.size, dtype=int)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(F, -top_m)[-top_m:]
        vals = F[idx]
        maxv = float(np.max(vals))
        minv = float(np.min(vals))
        span = maxv - minv
        if span < 1e-9:
            w = np.full(idx.size, 1.0 / float(idx.size), dtype=float)
        else:
            tau = max(span * 0.35, 1e-8)
            w = np.exp((vals - maxv) / tau)
            w /= float(np.sum(w))

        r = compute_active_r(h, idx, w, n)

        br = np.where((h < 1.0 - 1e-11) & (h > 1e-11))[0]
        if br.size == 0:
            break

        if br.size <= pool:
            pos_pool = br.copy()
        else:
            pp = np.argpartition(r[br], pool)[:pool]
            pos_pool = br[pp]
            pos_pool = pos_pool[np.argsort(r[pos_pool])]

        if br.size <= pool:
            neg_pool = br.copy()
        else:
            nn = np.argpartition(r[br], -pool)[-pool:]
            neg_pool = br[nn]
            neg_pool = neg_pool[np.argsort(-r[neg_pool])]

        best_pair = None
        best_val = M

        for ii in pos_pool:
            ii = int(ii)
            if h[ii] <= 1e-11 or h[ii] >= 1.0 - 1e-11:
                continue
            for jj in neg_pool:
                jj = int(jj)
                if ii == jj or h[jj] <= 1e-11 or h[jj] >= 1.0 - 1e-11:
                    continue
                out = pair_line_search(F, h, ii, jj, M)
                if out is not None:
                    d, val = out
                    if val < best_val - 1e-11:
                        best_val = val
                        best_pair = (ii, jj, d)

        if best_pair is None:
            if br.size > 25:
                bp = np.argpartition(r[br], 25)[:25]
                bpos = br[bp]
                bn = np.argpartition(r[br], -25)[-25:]
                bneg = br[bn]
            else:
                bpos = br
                bneg = br
            if bpos.size > 0 and bneg.size > 0:
                for _ in range(10):
                    ii = int(bpos[int(rng.integers(bpos.size))])
                    jj = int(bneg[int(rng.integers(bneg.size))])
                    if ii == jj or h[ii] <= 1e-11 or h[jj] <= 1e-11:
                        continue
                    out = pair_line_search(F, h, ii, jj, M)
                    if out is not None:
                        d, val = out
                        if val < best_val - 1e-11:
                            best_val = val
                            best_pair = (ii, jj, d)

        if best_pair is not None:
            ii, jj, d = best_pair
            apply_pair(h, F, ii, jj, d)
            M = float(np.max(F))
            if M * dx < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = M * dx
                best_h = h.copy()
            no_improve = 0

            if (it % 250 == 0) or (time.perf_counter() - t_last_recalc > 25.0):
                ssum = float(np.sum(h))
                if abs(ssum - target) > 1e-8:
                    h = project_density(h, target)
                    F = compute_F_all(h)
                    M = float(np.max(F))
                else:
                    F = compute_F_all(h)
                    M = float(np.max(F))
                t_last_recalc = time.perf_counter()
                if M * dx < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = M * dx
                    best_h = h.copy()
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve % 18 == 0:
                hb, Fb, Mb = batch_step(h, F, r, M)
                if hb is not None:
                    h = hb
                    F = Fb
                    M = Mb
                    if M * dx < best_c - 1e-12:
                        best_c = M * dx
                        best_h = h.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
            if no_improve > 80:
                improved = False
                for sigma in (0.035, 0.022, 0.012, 0.006):
                    hp = project_density(h + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n), target)
                    Fp = compute_F_all(hp)
                    Mp = float(np.max(Fp))
                    if Mp < M - 1e-9:
                        h = hp
                        F = Fp
                        M = Mp
                        if M * dx < best_c - 1e-12:
                            best_c = M * dx
                            best_h = h.copy()
                        no_improve = 0
                        improved = True
                        break
                if not improved and no_improve > 500:
                    break

    return best_h, best_c


def make_basis(n, kind):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    z = x - 1.0
    z2 = z * z
    B = []
    if kind == 0:
        for m in range(1, 7):
            B.append(np.cos(np.pi * float(m) * x, dtype=float))
    elif kind == 1:
        B.append(z.copy())
        B.append(0.5 * (3.0 * z2 - 1.0))
        B.append(0.5 * (5.0 * z2 * z - 3.0 * z))
        B.append((35.0 * z2 * z2 - 30.0 * z2 + 3.0) / 8.0)
        B.append((63.0 * z2 * z2 * z - 70.0 * z2 * z + 15.0 * z) / 8.0)
        B.append((231.0 * z2 * z2 * z2 - 315.0 * z2 * z2 + 105.0 * z2 - 5.0) / 16.0)
    elif kind == 2:
        for m in (1, 3, 5, 7, 9):
            B.append(np.cos(np.pi * float(m) * x, dtype=float))
    else:
        B.append(z2 - 1.0 / 3.0)
        B.append(z2 * z2 - 2.0 / 5.0)
        B.append(z2 * z2 * z2 - 2.0 / 7.0)
    return B


def make_h_basis(raw, B, norms, n):
    denom = float(np.sum(np.abs(raw) * norms))
    if denom <= 1e-14:
        return project_density(np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float), 0.5 * n)
    scale = 0.5 / denom
    f = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for j in range(len(B)):
        f += (scale * float(raw[j])) * B[j]
    h = 0.5 + f
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_density(h, 0.5 * n)


def basis_random_candidates(rng, n_eval, deadline):
    n_eval = int(n_eval)
    bases = []
    for kind in range(4):
        B = make_basis(n_eval, kind)
        norms = np.array([float(np.max(np.abs(b))) for b in B], dtype=float)
        bases.append((B, norms))

    cands = []

    for B, norms in bases:
        mb = len(B)
        for s in range(7):
            raw = np.zeros(mb, dtype=float)
            if s == 0:
                raw[0] = 1.0
            elif s == 1:
                raw[min(1, mb - 1)] = 1.0
            elif s == 2:
                raw[0] = 0.85
                if mb > 1:
                    raw[1] = -0.32
            elif s == 3:
                raw[:] = np.exp(-np.arange(mb, dtype=float) / 1.2)
            elif s == 4:
                raw[:] = np.exp(-np.arange(mb, dtype=float) / 1.5)
                signs = np.ones(mb, dtype=float)
                signs[1::2] = -1.0
                raw *= signs
            elif s == 5:
                raw[0] = 1.0
                if mb > 2:
                    raw[2] = 0.35
            else:
                raw[0] = 0.6
                if mb > 1:
                    raw[1] = 0.5
            h = make_h_basis(raw, B, norms, n_eval)
            c = evaluate_c(h)
            if np.isfinite(c) and c < 0.46:
                cands.append((c, h))

    bi = 0
    while time.perf_counter() + 1.0 < deadline:
        B, norms = bases[bi % 4]
        bi += 1

        if len(cands) > 7000:
            vals = np.array([cc for cc, _ in cands], dtype=float)
            order = np.argsort(vals)[:2000]
            cands = [cands[int(i)] for i in order]

        mb = len(B)
        if bi % 9 == 0:
            raw = np.zeros(mb, dtype=float)
            raw[int(rng.integers(mb))] = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
        elif bi % 13 == 0:
            raw = np.zeros(mb, dtype=float)
            raw[0] = float(rng.uniform(0.4, 1.0))
            if mb > 1:
                raw[1] = float(rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6))
        else:
            decay = np.exp(-np.arange(mb, dtype=float) / float(rng.uniform(0.8, 2.8)))
            raw = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=mb) * decay

        h = make_h_basis(raw, B, norms, n_eval)
        c = evaluate_c(h)
        if np.isfinite(c) and c < 0.46:
            if len(cands) < 8000:
                cands.append((c, h))

    if cands:
        vals = np.array([cc for cc, _ in cands], dtype=float)
        order = np.argsort(vals)[:12]
        cands = [cands[int(i)] for i in order]
    return cands


def block_random_candidates(rng, n_eval, deadline):
    n_eval = int(n_eval)
    cands = []
    x = (np.arange(n_eval) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_eval))
    while time.perf_counter() + 1.0 < deadline and len(cands) < 600:
        m = int(rng.integers(3, 9))
        edges = np.sort(rng.random(m - 1) * 2.0)
        edges = np.concatenate(([0.0], edges, [2.0]))
        idx = np.searchsorted(edges, x, side="right") - 1
        vals = rng.random(m)
        h = vals[idx]
        h = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
        h = project_density(h, 0.5 * n_eval)
        c = evaluate_c(h)
        if np.isfinite(c) and c < 0.45:
            cands.append((c, h))

    if cands:
        vals = np.array([cc for cc, _ in cands], dtype=float)
        order = np.argsort(vals)[:8]
        cands = [cands[int(i)] for i in order]
    return cands


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.perf_counter()
    budget = float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 1300.0
    budget = max(30.0, budget)
    deadline = start + max(5.0, budget - 25.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")
    best_n = 0

    try:
        raw = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if raw.ndim == 1 and raw.size >= 2:
            raw = np.nan_to_num(raw, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
            raw = np.clip(raw, 0.0, 1.0)
            h0 = project_density(raw, 0.5 * raw.size)
            c0 = evaluate_c(h0)
            if np.isfinite(c0):
                best_h = h0
                best_c = c0
                best_n = int(h0.size)
    except Exception:
        best_h = None

    if best_h is None:
        n = 450
        x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
        h = 0.5 + 0.68 * np.cos(np.pi * x)
        h = project_density(h, 0.5 * n)
        best_h = h
        best_c = evaluate_c(h)
        best_n = int(n)

    parent_h = best_h.copy()
    parent_n = int(best_h.size)

    rem = deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if rem > 120:
        dl = time.perf_counter() + min(170.0, rem * 0.16)
        h1, c1 = coordinate_local(parent_h, dl, rng)
        if c1 < best_c - 1e-13:
            best_h = h1
            best_c = c1
            best_n = int(h1.size)

    if budget > 1000:
        n_fine = 900
    elif budget > 600:
        n_fine = 720
    else:
        n_fine = max(360, parent_n)
    if n_fine % 2 != 0:
        n_fine += 1
    n_fine = max(360, min(1080, n_fine))

    base_fine = upsample_h(best_h, n_fine)
    base_fine_c = evaluate_c(base_fine)
    if base_fine_c < best_c - 1e-13:
        best_h = base_fine
        best_c = base_fine_c
        best_n = int(base_fine.size)

    fine_start_h = base_fine.copy()
    fine_start_c = base_fine_c
    all_cands = []

    rem = deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if rem > 240:
        n_coarse = min(max(360, parent_n), n_fine)
        if n_coarse % 2 != 0:
            n_coarse += 1
        cand_dl = time.perf_counter() + min(230.0, rem * 0.32)
        all_cands.extend(basis_random_candidates(rng, n_coarse, cand_dl))

        rem = deadline - time.perf_counter()
        if rem > 120:
            block_dl = time.perf_counter() + min(70.0, rem * 0.22)
            all_cands.extend(block_random_candidates(rng, n_coarse, block_dl))

        for c, hcoarse in all_cands:
            if c < best_c - 1e-13:
                best_h = hcoarse.copy()
                best_c = c
                best_n = int(hcoarse.size)
            hf = upsample_h(hcoarse, n_fine)
            cf = evaluate_c(hf)
            if cf < best_c - 1e-13:
                best_h = hf.copy()
                best_c = cf
                best_n = n_fine
            if cf < fine_start_c - 1e-12:
                fine_start_c = cf
                fine_start_h = hf.copy()

    if time.perf_counter() < deadline - 120:
        if fine_start_h is None or fine_start_h.size != n_fine:
            fine_start_h = upsample_h(best_h, n_fine)
        else:
            fine_start_h = project_density(fine_start_h, 0.5 * n_fine)

        h2, c2 = coordinate_local(fine_start_h, deadline - 2.0, rng)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2
            best_n = int(h2.size)

    rem = deadline - time.perf_counter()
    if rem > 60 and best_h is not None and int(best_h.size) != n_fine:
        h3, c3 = coordinate_local(best_h, deadline - 2.0, rng)
        if c3 < best_c - 1e-13:
            best_h = h3
            best_c = c3
            best_n = int(h3.size)

    final_h = project_density(best_h, 0.5 * int(best_h.size))
    final_c = evaluate_c(final_h)
    return final_h, float(final_c), int(final_h.size)
```
